_bf16_gemm_kernel:
.text._bf16_gemm_kernel:
        /*0000*/                   LDC R1, c[0x0][0x37c] ;
        /*0010*/                   LDC.64 R118, c[0x0][0x398] ;
        /*0020*/                   S2R R7, SR_CTAID.X ;
        /*0030*/                   LDCU UR8, c[0x0][0x3a8] ;
        /*0040*/                   LOP3.LUT R0, R0, 0xfffffffb, RZ, 0xc0, !PT ;
        /*0050*/                   S2R R72, SR_TID.X ;
        /*0060*/                   LDCU.128 UR4, c[0x0][0x380] ;
        /*0070*/                   MOV R27, RZ ;
        /*0080*/                   LDC.64 R126, c[0x0][0x3a0] ;
        /*0090*/                   LDCU.64 UR12, c[0x0][0x358] ;
        /*00a0*/                   IADD3 R2, PT, PT, R119, 0x7f, RZ ;
        /*00b0*/                   SHF.R.S32.HI R3, RZ, 0x1f, R2 ;
        /*00c0*/                   IADD3 R124, PT, PT, R126, 0x3f, RZ ;
        /*00d0*/                   LEA.HI R3, R3, R2, RZ, 0x7 ;
        /*00e0*/                   IABS R8, R7 ;
        /*00f0*/                   SHF.R.S32.HI R3, RZ, 0x7, R3 ;

        /*0110*/                   LOP3.LUT R28, R72, 0x7, RZ, 0xc0, !PT ;
        /*0120*/                   LOP3.LUT R165, R72, 0x60, RZ, 0xc0, !PT ;
        /*0130*/                   IMAD.SHL.U32 R4, R3, 0x8, RZ ;
        /*0140*/                   IMAD.SHL.U32 R164, R28, 0x8, RZ ;
        /*0150*/                   IABS R9, R4.reuse ;
        /*0160*/                   IABS R10, R4 ;
        /*0170*/                   I2F.RP R5, R9 ;
        /*0180*/                   MUFU.RCP R5, R5 ;
        /*0190*/                   IADD3 R2, PT, PT, R5, 0xffffffe, RZ ;
        /*01a0*/                   IADD3 R5, PT, PT, RZ, -R10, RZ ;
        /*01b0*/                   F2I.FTZ.U32.TRUNC.NTZ R3, R2 ;
        /*01c0*/                   MOV R2, RZ ;
        /*01d0*/                   IADD3 R6, PT, PT, RZ, -R3, RZ ;
        /*01e0*/                   IMAD R11, R6, R9, RZ ;

        /*0250*/              @!P1 IADD3 R3, PT, PT, R3, 0x1, RZ ;

        /*02d0*/                   SHF.R.S32.HI R3, RZ, 0x1f, R2 ;

        /*0320*/                   IADD3 R9, PT, PT, -R9, RZ, RZ ;
        /*0330*/                   LEA.HI.SX32 R3, R3, -R11, 0x1a ;
        /*0340*/                   IMAD R4, R4, R9, R7 ;

        /*04d0*/                   IMAD R3, R3, R9, R6 ;
